    /* Hide body until the full stylesheet loads and overrides this with visibility: visible.
       Background colours are still set here because body's background propagates to the
       viewport canvas even when visibility: hidden, preventing a dark-mode white flash. */
    body { background-color: #F6F8FC; visibility: hidden; }
    .dark body { background-color: #202020; }
    /* Side panel must be off-screen before body is revealed — without this, the CSS
       transition animates it from on-screen to off-screen on every page load.
       NOTE: This ID rule has higher specificity than Tailwind classes, so openSidePanel()
       must clear element.style.transform before removing the -translate-x-full class. */
    #side-bar-menu { transform: translateX(-100%); }
  </style>

  <div id="most-searched-editor-modal" class="hidden fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center" style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);" onclick="if(event.target===this) this.style.display='none'">
    <div class="bg-white dark:bg-background-dark rounded-lg shadow-xl w-[500px] max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col" onclick="event.stopPropagation()"
         data-i18n-manual-mode="Manual mode"
         data-i18n-days-left-one="1 day left"
         data-i18n-days-left-other="0 days left"
         data-i18n-auto-refresh-active="Auto-refresh active"
         data-i18n-save-confirm="Save changes?"
         data-i18n-add-item="+ Add"
         data-i18n-no-results="No results found">
      <div class="flex justify-between items-center px-4 py-3 border-b border-light-border dark:border-dark-border shrink-0">
        <span class="font-bold text-lg">Edit Top Searches</span>
        <span class="cursor-pointer text-2xl leading-none" onclick="document.getElementById('most-searched-editor-modal').style.display='none'">&times;</span>
      </div>
      <div id="ms-lock-bar" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm border-b border-light-border dark:border-dark-border shrink-0" style="background: #f8f9fa;">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
          <span class="font-medium text-gray-600">Auto-refresh setting</span>
          <span id="ms-mode-badge" class="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded"></span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
            <input type="radio" name="ms-lock-mode" value="always_manual" style="accent-color: var(--color-primary);" onchange="msApplyMode()" />
            <span>Always manual</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">- I control this, never auto-refresh</span>
          </label>
          <label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
            <input type="radio" name="ms-lock-mode" value="days" style="accent-color: var(--color-primary);" onchange="msApplyMode()" />
            <span>Keep for</span>
            <input id="ms-lock-days" type="number" min="1" max="90" value="7" class="w-14 px-1 py-0.5 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded text-center" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); document.querySelector('input[name=ms-lock-mode][value=days]').checked=true; msApplyMode();" />
            <span>days</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">- then auto-refresh resumes</span>
          </label>
          <label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
            <input type="radio" name="ms-lock-mode" value="auto" style="accent-color: var(--color-primary);" onchange="msApplyMode()" />
            <span>Auto-refresh</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">- system manages this automatically</span>
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0">
        <div class="px-4 py-3">
          <div class="flex gap-2 mb-3">
            <input id="ms-search-input" type="text" placeholder="Search stores or groups to add..." class="flex-1 p-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 dark:border-dark-border rounded dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white" />
            <button onclick="msSearchItems()" class="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white rounded" style="background-color: var(--color-primary);">Search</button>
          </div>
          <div id="ms-search-results" class="mb-3 max-h-[150px] overflow-y-auto hidden border border-gray-200 dark:border-dark-border rounded"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="px-4 pb-2">
          <div class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 mb-2">Current items (drag to reorder):</div>
        </div>
        <div id="ms-current-items" class="px-4 pb-3"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="flex justify-end gap-2 px-4 py-3 border-t border-light-border dark:border-dark-border shrink-0">
        <button onclick="document.getElementById('most-searched-editor-modal').style.display='none'" class="px-4 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded">Cancel</button>
        <button onclick="msSaveItems()" class="px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white rounded" style="background-color: var(--color-primary);">Save</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <script>
    var msItems = [];
    var msCurrentMode = 'auto';
    var msModal = document.getElementById('most-searched-editor-modal').querySelector('[data-i18n-manual-mode]');
    var msI18n = {
      manualMode: msModal.dataset.i18nManualMode,
      daysLeftOne: msModal.dataset.i18nDaysLeftOne,
      daysLeftOther: msModal.dataset.i18nDaysLeftOther,
      autoRefreshActive: msModal.dataset.i18nAutoRefreshActive,
      saveConfirm: msModal.dataset.i18nSaveConfirm,
      addItem: msModal.dataset.i18nAddItem,
      noResults: msModal.dataset.i18nNoResults
    };

    function msLoadEditor() {
      fetch('/most_searched_editor', { headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' } })
        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
        .then(function(data) {
          msItems = data.items || [];
          msRenderItems();
          msCurrentMode = data.lock_mode || 'auto';
          msRenderMode(data.lock_mode, data.days_left);
        });
    }

    function msRenderMode(mode, daysLeft) {
      var radios = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="ms-lock-mode"]');
      radios.forEach(function(r) { r.checked = (r.value === mode); });
      var badge = document.getElementById('ms-mode-badge');
      badge.textContent = '';
      badge.style.padding = '2px 8px';
      badge.style.borderRadius = '4px';
      if (mode === 'always_manual') {
        badge.style.color = '#856404';
        badge.style.background = '#fff3cd';
        badge.textContent = msI18n.manualMode;
      } else if (mode === 'days') {
        badge.style.color = '#0c5460';
        badge.style.background = '#d1ecf1';
        badge.textContent = (daysLeft === 1 ? msI18n.daysLeftOne : msI18n.daysLeftOther).replace('%{count}', daysLeft);
      } else {
        badge.style.color = '#155724';
        badge.style.background = '#d4edda';
        badge.textContent = msI18n.autoRefreshActive;
      }
      msCurrentMode = mode;
    }

    function msApplyMode() {
      var selected = document.querySelector('input[name="ms-lock-mode"]:checked');
      if (!selected) return;
      var mode = selected.value;
      var days = parseInt(document.getElementById('ms-lock-days').value) || 7;
      fetch('/toggle_most_searched_lock', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content },
        body: JSON.stringify({ mode: mode, days: days })
      })
        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
        .then(function(data) {
          if (data.success) {
            msRenderMode(data.lock_mode, data.days_left);
          } else {
            alert('Error: ' + (data.error || data.message));
          }
        });
    }

    function msRenderItems() {
      var container = document.getElementById('ms-current-items');
      container.textContent = '';
      msItems.forEach(function(item, index) {
        var name = item.class_name === 'Merchant' ? item.merchant_name : item.name;
        var div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'flex justify-between items-center p-2 mb-1 border border-gray-200 dark:border-dark-border rounded cursor-move';
        div.draggable = true;
        div.dataset.index = index;
        div.ondragstart = function(e) { e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', index); };
        div.ondragover = function(e) { e.preventDefault(); this.style.borderColor = 'var(--color-primary)'; };
        div.ondragleave = function() { this.style.borderColor = ''; };
        div.ondrop = function(e) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.style.borderColor = '';
          var from = parseInt(e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'));
          var to = parseInt(this.dataset.index);
          var moved = msItems.splice(from, 1)[0];
          msItems.splice(to, 0, moved);
          msRenderItems();
        };

        var info = document.createElement('div');
        info.className = 'flex items-center gap-2 text-sm';
        var img = document.createElement('img');
        img.src = item.photo_medium || '';
        img.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded';
        img.alt = name;
        var span = document.createElement('span');
        span.textContent = name + ' (' + item.class_name + ')';
        info.appendChild(img);
        info.appendChild(span);

        var removeBtn = document.createElement('button');
        removeBtn.textContent = '\u00d7';
        removeBtn.className = 'text-red-500 font-bold text-lg leading-none px-1';
        removeBtn.onclick = function() { msItems.splice(index, 1); msRenderItems(); };

        div.appendChild(info);
        div.appendChild(removeBtn);
        container.appendChild(div);
      });
    }

    function msSearchItems() {
      var q = document.getElementById('ms-search-input').value.trim();
      if (q.length < 2) return;
      fetch('/merchant_group_lookup?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q), { headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' } })
        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
        .then(function(data) {
          var container = document.getElementById('ms-search-results');
          container.textContent = '';
          container.classList.remove('hidden');
          var all = (data.merchants || []).concat(data.groups || []);
          if (all.length === 0) {
            container.textContent = msI18n.noResults;
            container.className += ' p-2 text-sm text-gray-500';
            return;
          }
          all.forEach(function(item) {
            var exists = msItems.some(function(i) { return i.id === item.id && i.class_name === item.type; });
            if (exists) return;
            var div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'flex justify-between items-center p-2 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 cursor-pointer text-sm';
            div.onclick = function() {
              var newItem = {
                id: item.id,
                permalink: item.url ? item.url.split('/').pop() : '',
                photo_medium: item.image_url || '',
                class_name: item.type
              };
              if (item.type === 'Merchant') { newItem.merchant_name = item.name; }
              else { newItem.name = item.name; }
              msItems.push(newItem);
              msRenderItems();
              container.classList.add('hidden');
              document.getElementById('ms-search-input').value = '';
            };
            var text = document.createElement('span');
            text.textContent = item.name + ' (' + item.type + ')';
            var addSpan = document.createElement('span');
            addSpan.textContent = msI18n.addItem;
            addSpan.style.color = 'var(--color-primary)';
            addSpan.className = 'font-medium text-xs';
            div.appendChild(text);
            div.appendChild(addSpan);
            container.appendChild(div);
          });
        });
    }

    function msSaveItems() {
      if (!confirm(msI18n.saveConfirm)) return;
      fetch('/update_most_searched', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-Token': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content },
        body: JSON.stringify({ items: msItems })
      })
        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
        .then(function(data) {
          if (data.success) {
            alert(data.message);
            document.getElementById('most-searched-editor-modal').style.display='none';
            location.reload();
          } else {
            alert('Error: ' + data.message);
          }
        });
    }

    var editBtn = document.getElementById('most-searched-edit-btn');
    if (editBtn) {
      editBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        var modal = document.getElementById('most-searched-editor-modal');
        if (modal.parentElement !== document.body) document.body.appendChild(modal);
        modal.classList.remove('hidden');
        modal.style.display = 'flex';
        msLoadEditor();
      });
    }
  </script>

<script>
  let profile_drop = document.getElementById("profile-drop");
  let profile_drop_content = document.getElementById("profile-drop-content");
  
  if(profile_drop){
      profile_drop.addEventListener("click", () => {
      profile_drop_content.classList.toggle("open");
      if (!profile_drop_content.classList.contains("open")) {
        profile_drop_content.style.height = "0px";
        profile_drop.style.borderBottomRightRadius = "";
        profile_drop.style.borderBottomLeftRadius = "";
        profile_drop_content.classList.add("hidden");
        
      } else {
        profile_drop_content.classList.remove("hidden");
        profile_drop_content.style.height = `${profile_drop_content.scrollHeight + "px"}`;
        profile_drop.style.borderBottomRightRadius = "0px";
        profile_drop.style.borderBottomLeftRadius = "0px";
      }
        let arrowIcon = event.currentTarget.querySelector(".iconrot");
        arrowIcon.classList.toggle("-rotate-180");
      
    });
  }
</script>
